p3[5] & g3[4]) | (p3[7] & p3[6] & p3[5] & p3[4] & g3[3]) | (p3[7] & p3[6] & p3[5] & p3[4] & p3[3] & g3[2]) | (p3[7] & p3[6] & p3[5] & p3[4] & p3[3] & p3[2] & g3[1]) | (p3[7] & p3[6] & p3[5] & p3[4] & p3[3] & p3[2] & p3[1] & g3[0]);\n    assign block_propagate[3] = &p3;\n    assign block_carry[3] = block_generate[3] | (block_propagate[3] & block_carry[2]);\n    assign sum[31:24] = p3 ^ c3;\n\nendmodule\n\n\nmodule tpu_pe\n  #(parameter DW = 8,   // operand width (signed)\n    parameter ACCW = 32) // accumulator / partial-sum width (signed)\n   (\n    input                   clk,\n    input                   rst,      // synchronous, active high\n    input                   ld,       // weight-load shift enable\n    input                   en,       // compute enable\n    input [DW-1:0]          w_north,  // weight in from PE above\n    input [DW-1:0]          a_west,   // activation in from west\n    input [ACCW-1:0]        psum_north, // partial sum in from above\n    output [DW-1:0]         w_south,  // stored weight out to PE below\n    output reg [DW-1:0]     a_east,   // activation out to east\n    output reg [ACCW-1:0]   psum_south // partial sum out to below\n    );\n\n   reg [DW-1:0] w;\n   assign w_south = w;\n\n   wire signed [DW-1:0] a_signed = a_west;\n   wire signed [DW-1:0] w_signed = w;\n   wire signed [2*DW-1:0] prod = a_signed * w_signed;\n   wire [ACCW-1:0] prod_sext = {{(ACCW-2*DW){prod[2*DW-1]}}, prod};\n\n   wire [ACCW-1:0] sum;\n   carry_skip_adder_32 u_adder (\n       .a   (psum_north),\n       .b   (prod_sext),\n       .sum (sum)\n   );\n\n   always @(posedge clk) begin\n      if (rst) begin\n         w          <= {DW{1'b0}};\n         a_east     <= {DW{1'b0}};\n         psum_south <= {ACCW{1'b0}};\n      end\n      else begin\n         if (ld)\n           w <= w_north;\n         if (en) begin\n            a_east     <= a_west;\n            psum_south <= sum;\n         end\n      end\n   end\n\nendmodule\n\n\n//#############################################################################\n// N x N weight-stationary systolic array (the TPU MXU core).\n//#############################################################################\n\nmodule tpu_array\n  #(parameter N = 8,   // array dimension (N x N PEs)\n    parameter DW = 8,   // operand width (signed)\n    parameter ACCW = 32) // accumulator width (signed)\n   (\n    input                   clk,\n    input                   rst,  // synchronous, active high\n    input                   ld,   // weight-load shift enable\n    input                   en,   // compute enable\n    input [N*DW-1:0]        w_top, // weight inputs, one per column (load)\n    input [N*DW-1:0]        a_left, // activation inputs, one per row\n    output [N*ACCW-1:0]     c_bot  // column results, one per column\n    );\n\n   wire [DW-1:0]   a_h    [0:N-1][0:N];\n   wire [ACCW-1:0] psum_v [0:N][0:N-1];\n   wire [DW-1:0]   w_v    [0:N][0:N-1];\n\n   genvar          i, j;\n   generate\n      for (i = 0; i < N; i = i + 1) begin : g_west\n         assign a_h[i][0] = a_left[i*DW +: DW];\n      end\n      for (j = 0; j < N; j = j + 1) begin : g_edge\n         assign psum_v[0][j] = {ACCW{1'b0}};\n         assign w_v[0][j]    = w_top[j*DW +: DW];\n         assign c_bot[j*ACCW +: ACCW] = (.clk        (clk),\n               .rst        (rst),\n               .ld         (ld),\n               .en         (en),\n               .w_north    (w_v[i][j]),\n               .a_west end\n      end\n   endgenerate\n\nendmodule\n\n\n//#############################################################################\n// Weight-stationary systolic matrix-multiply tile (a Google-TPU-style MXU).\n//#############################################################################\n\nmodule tpu\n  #(parameter N = 8,   // array dimension (N x N)\n    parameter DW = 8,   // operand width (signed)\n    parameter ACCW = 32) // result / accumulator width (signed)\n   (\n    input                   clk,\n    input                   rst,   // synchronous, active high\n    input                   w_valid, // weight-row valid (load phase)\n    input [N*DW-1:0]        w_data,  // weight row, packed { col N-1 ... col 0 }\n    input                   a_valid, // activation-row valid (compute phase)\n    input [N*DW-1:0]        a_data,  // activation row, packed { col N-1 ... col 0 }\n    output                  c_valid, // result-row valid\n    output [N*ACCW-1:0]     c_data   // result row, packed { col N-1 ... col 0 }\n    );\n\n   localparam LAT = 2*N - 1;\n\n   wire      ld = w_valid;\n   wire      en = ~w_valid;\n\n   // Input skew\n   wire [N*DW-1:0] a_left;\n   genvar          i;\n   generate\n      for (i = 0; i < N; i = i + 1) begin : g_skew\n         wire [DW-1:0] din =\n               a_valid ? a_data[i*DW +: DW] : {DW{1'b0}};\n         if (i == 0) begin : g_skew0\n            assign a_left[i*DW +: DW] = din;\n         end\n         else begin : g_skewn\n            reg [DW-1:0] sr [0:i-1];\n            integer k;\n            always @(posedge clk) begin\n               if (rst) begin\n                  for (k = 0; k < i; k = k + 1)\n                    sr[k] <= {DW{1'b0}};\n               end\n               else if (en) begin\n                  sr[0] <= din;\n                  for (k = 1; k < i; k = k + 1)\n                    sr[k] <= sr[k-1];\n               end\n            end\n            assign a_left[i*DW +: DW] = sr[i-1];\n         end\n      end\n   endgenerate\n\n   // Systolic array\n   wire [N*ACCW-1:0] c_bot;\n   tpu_array #(.N(N), .DW(DW), .ACCW(ACCW)) u_array\n     (.clk(clk), .rst(rst), .ld(ld), .en(en),\n      .w_top(w_data), .a_left(a_left), .c_bot(c_bot));\n\n   // Output de-skew\n   genvar j;\n   generate\n      for (j = 0; j < N; j = j + 1) begin : g_deskew\n         localparam D = N - 1 - j;\n         wire [ACCW-1:0] cin = c_bot[j*ACCW +: ACCW];\n         if (D == 0) begin : g_deskew0\n            assign c_data[j*ACCW +: ACCW] = cin;\n         end\n         else begin : g_deskewn\n            reg [ACCW-1:0] dr [0:D-1];\n            integer k;\n            always @(posedge clk) begin\n               if (rst) begin\n                  for (k = 0; k < D; k = k + 1)\n                    dr[k] <= {ACCW{1'b0}};\n      end\n            assign c_data[j*ACCW +: ACCW] = dr[D-1];\n         end\n      end\n   endgenerate\n\n   // Valid pipeline\n   reg [LAT-1:0] vpipe;\n   always @(posedge clk) begin\n      if (rst)\n        vpipe <= {LAT{1'b0}};\n      else if (en)\n        vpipe <= {vpipe[LAT-2:0], a_valid};\n   end\n   assign c_valid = vpipe[LAT-1];\n\nendmodule")

adder\n// The key insight: register the multiplier OUTPUT, not inputs\n// This maintains cycle accuracy while breaking the critical path\n//#############################################################################\n\nmodule tpu_pe\n  #(parameter DW = 8,   // operand width (signed)\n    parameter ACCW = 32) // accumulator / partial-sum width (signed)\n   (\n    input                   clk,\n    input                   rst,      // synchronous, active high\n    input                   ld,       // weight-load shift enable\n    input                   en,       // compute enable\n    input [DW-1:0]          w_north,  // weight in from PE above\n    input [DW-1:0]          a_west,   // activation in from west\n    input [ACCW-1:0]        psum_north, // partial sum in from above\n    output [DW-1:0]         w_south,  // stored weight out to PE below\n    output reg [DW-1:0]     a_east,   // activation out to east\n    output reg [ACCW-1:0]   psum_south // partial sum out to below\n    );\n\n   reg [DW-1:0] w;\n   assign w_south = w;\n\n   // Register the product to break the multiply-add chain\n   wire signed [DW-1:0] a_signed = a_west;\n   wire signed [DW-1:0] w_signed = w;\n   wire signed [2*DW-1:0] prod = a_signed * w_signed;\n   wire [ACCW-1:0] prod_sext = {{(ACCW-2*DW){prod[2*DW-1]}}, prod};\n a_east     <= a_west;\n            prod_reg   <= prod_sext;\n            psum_south <= sum;\n         end\n      end\n   end\n\nendmodule\n\n\n//#############################################################################\n// N x N weight-stationary systolic array (the TPU MXU core).\n//#############################################################################\n\nmodule tpu_array\n  #(parameter N = 8,   // array dimension (N x N PEs)\n    parameter DW = 8,   // operand    end\n      for (j = 0; j < N; j = j + 1) begin : g_edge\n         assign psum_v[0][j] = {ACCW{1'b0}};\n         assign w_v[0][j]    = w_top[j*DW +: DW];\n         assign c_bot[j*ACCW +: ACCW] = MXU).\n//#############################################################################\n\nmodule tpu\n  #(parameter N = 8,   // array dimension (N x N)\n    parameter DW = 8,   // operand width   -> create_file(filename='design_opt16.sv', content="//#############################################################################\n// Optimized TPU - using array multiplier with carry-save adder tree\n//#############################################################################\n\nmodule array_mult_8x8 (\n    input  [7:0]  a,\n    input  [7:0]  b,\n    output [15:0] prod\n);\n    // Array multiplier with Booth encoding (radix-4)\n    logic signed [7:0] a_s, b_s;\n    assign a_s = a;\n    assign b_s = b;\n    \n    // Booth radix-4 partial products (4 partial products for 8 bits)\n    logic [15:0] pp [0:3];\n    logic [2:0]  booth [0:3];\n    \n    assign booth[0] = {b_s[1], b_s[0], 1'b0};\n    assign booth[1] = {b_s[3], b_s[2], b_s[1]};\n    assign booth[2] = {b_s[5], b_s[4], b_s[3]};\n    assign booth[3] = {b_s[7], b_s[6], b_s[5]};\n    \n    genvar k;\n    generate\n        for (k = 0; k < 4; k++) begin : gen_pp\n            always_comb begin\n                case (booth[k])\n                    3'b000: pp[k] = 16'sb0;\n                    3'b001: pp[k] = {{8{a_s[7]}}, a_s};\n                    3'b010: pp[k] = {{8{a_s[7]}}, a_s};\n                    3'b011: pp[k] = {{7{a_s[7]}}, a_s, 1'b0};\n                    3'b100: pp[k] = -({{7{a_s[7]}}, a_s, 1'b0});\n                    3'b101: pp[k] = -({{8{a_s[7]}}, a_s});\n                    3'b110: pp[k] = -({{8{a_s[7]}}, a_s});\n                    3'b111: pp[k] = 16'sb0;\n                endcase\n                pp[k] = pp[k] << (2*k);\n            end\n        end\n    endgenerate\n    \n    // Wallace tree reduction: 4:2 compressor\n    logic [15:0] s1, c1;\n    logic [15:0] s2, c2;\n    \n    // First reduction: pp[0] + pp[1] + pp[2] -> s1, c1\n    assign {c1, s1} = pp[0] + pp[1] + pp[2];\n    \n    // Second reduction: s1 + (c1<<1) + pp[3] -> s2, c2\n    assign {c2, s2} = s1 + (c1 << 1) + pp[3];\n    \n    // Final addition\n    assign prod = s2 + (c2 << 1);\n\nendmodule\n\n\nmodule tpu_pe\n  #(parameter DW = 8,   // operand width (signed)\n    parameter ACCW = 32) // accumulator / partial-sum width (signed)\n   (\n    input                   clk,\n    input                   rst,      // synchronous, active high\n    input                   ld,       // weight-load shift enable\n    input                   en,       // compute enable\n    input [DW-1:0]          w_north,  // weight in from PE above\n    input [DW-1:0]          a_west,   // activation in from west\n    input [ACCW-1:0]        psum_north, // partial sum in from above\n    output [DW-1:0]         w_south,  // stored weight out to PE below\n    output reg [DW-1:0]     a_east,   // activation out to east\n    output reg [ACCW-1:0]   psum_south // partial sum out to below\n    );\n\n   reg [DW-1:0] w;\n   assign w_south = w;\n\n   wire [15:0] prod;\n   array_mult_8x8 u_mult (\n       .a   (a_west),\n       .b   (w),\n       .prod(prod)\n   );\n   \n   wire [ACCW-1:0] prod_sext = {{(ACCW-16){prod[15]}}, prod};\n   \n   // Simple adder - synthesis will optimize\n   wire [ACCW-1:0] sum = psum_north + prod_sext;\n\n   always @(posedge clk) begin\n      if (rst) begin\n         w          <= {DW{1'b0}};\n         a_east     <= {DW{1'b0}};\n         psum_south <= {ACCW{1'b0}};\n      end\n      else begin\n         if (ld)\n           w <= w_north;\n         if (en) begin\n            a_east     <= a_west;\n            psum_south <= sum;\n         end\n      end\n   end\n\nendmodule\n\n\n//#############################################################################\n// N x N weight-stationary systolic array (the TPU MXU core).\n//#############################################################################\n\nmodule tpu_array\n  #(parameter N = 8,   // array dimension (N x N PEs)\n    parameter DW = 8,   // operand width (signed)\n    parameter ACCW = 32) // accumulator width (signed)\n   (\n    input                   clk,\n    input                   rst,  // synchronous, active high\n    input                   ld,   // weight-load shift enable\n    input                   en,   // compute enable\n    input [N*DW-1:0]        w_top, // weight inputs, one per column (load)\n    input [N*DW-1:0]        a_left, // activation inputs, one per row\n    output [N*ACCW-1:0]     c_bot  // column results, one per column\n    );\n\n   wire [DW-1:0]   a_h    [0:N-1][0:N];\n   wire [ACCW-1:0] psum_v [0:N][0:N-1];\n   wire [DW-1:0]   w_v    [0:N][0:N-1];\n\n   genvar          i, j;\n   generate\n      for (i = 0; i < N; i = i + 1) begin : g_west\n         assign a_h[i][0] = a_left[i*DW +: DW];\n      end\n      for (j = 0; j < N; j = j + 1) begin : g_edge\n         assign psum_v[0][j] = {ACCW{1'b0}};\n         assign w_v[0][j]    = w_top[j*DW +: DW];\n         assign c_bot[j*ACCW +: ACCW] = psum_v[N][j];\n      end\n      for (i = 0; i < N; i = i + 1) begin : g_pe_row\n         for (j = 0; j < N; j = j + 1) begin : g_pe_col\n            tpu_pe #(.DW(DW), .ACCW(ACCW)) u_pe\n              (.clk        (clk),\n               .rst        (rst),\n               .ld         (ld),\n               .en         (en),\n               .w_north    (w_v[i][j]),\n               .a_west     (a_h[i][j]),\n               .psum_north (psum_v[i][j]),\n               .w_south    (w_v[i+1][j]),\n               .a_east     (a_h[i][j+1]),\n               .psum_south (psum_v[i+1][j]));\n         end\n      end\n   endgenerate\n\nendmodule\n\n\n//#############################################################################\n// Weight-stationary systolic matrix-multiply tile (a Google-TPU-style MXU).\n//#############################################################################\n\nmodule tpu\n  #(parameter N = 8,   // array dimension (N x N)\n    parameter DW = 8,   // operand width (signed)\n    parameter ACCW = 32) // result / accumulator width (signed)\n   (\n    input                   clk,\n    input                   rst,   // synchronous, active high\n    input                   w_valid, // weight-row valid (load phase)\n    input [N*DW-1:0]        w_data,  // weight row, packed { col N-1 ... col 0 }\n    input                   a_valid, // activation-row valid (compute phase)\n    input [N*DW-1:0]        a_data,  // activation row, packed { col N-1 ... col 0 }\n    output                  c_valid, // result-row valid\n    output [N*ACCW-1:0]     c_data   // result row, packed { col N-1 ... col 0 }\n    );\n\n   localparam LAT = 2*N - 1;\n\n   wire      ld = w_valid;\n   wire      en = ~w_valid;\n\n   // Input skew\n   wire [N*DW-1:0] a_left;\n   genvar          i;\n   generate\n      for (i = 0; i < N; i = i + 1) begin : g_skew\n         wire [DW-1:0] din =\n               a_valid ? a_data[i*DW +: DW] : {DW{1'b0}};\n         if (i == 0) begin : g_skew0\n            assign a_left[i*DW +: DW] = din;\n         end\n         else begin : g_skewn\n            reg [DW-1:0] sr [0:i-1];\n            integer k;\n            always @(posedge clk) begin\n               if (rst) begin\n                  for (k = 0; k < i; k = k + 1)\n                    sr[k] <= {DW{1'b0}};\n               end\n               else if (en) begin\n                  sr[0] <= din;\n                  for (k = 1; k < i; k = k + 1)\n                    sr[k] <= sr[k-1];\n               end\n            end\n            assign a_left[i*DW +: DW] = sr[i-1];\n         end\n      end\n   endgenerate\n\n   // Systolic array\n   wire [N*ACCW-1:0] c_bot;\n   tpu_array #(.N(N), .DW(DW), .ACCW(ACCW)) u_array\n     (.clk(clk), .rst(rst), .ld(ld), .en(en),\n      .w_top(w_data), .a_left(a_left), .c_bot(c_bot));\n\n   // Output de-skew\n   genvar j;\n   generate\n      for (j = 0; j < N; j = j + 1) begin : g_deskew\n         localparam D = N - 1 - j;\n         wire [ACCW-1:0] cin = c_bot[j*ACCW +: ACCW];\n         if (D == 0) begin : g_deskew0\n            assign c_data[j*ACCW +: ACCW] = cin;\n         end\n         else begin : g_deskewn\n            reg [ACCW-1:0] dr [0:D-1];\n            integer k;\n            always @(posedge clk) begin\n               if (rst) begin\n                  for (k = 0; k < D; k = k + 1)\n                    dr[k] <= {ACCW{1'b0}};\n               end\n               else if (en) begin\n                  dr[0] <= cin;\n                  for (k = 1; k < D; k = k + 1)\n                    dr[k] <= dr[k-1];\n               end\n            end\n            assign c_data[j*ACCW +: ACCW] = dr[D-1];\n         end\n      end\n   endgenerate\n\n   // Valid pipeline\n   reg [LAT-1:0] vpipe;\n   always @(posedge clk) begin\n      if (rst)\n        vpipe <= {LAT{1'b0}};\n      else if (en)\n        vpipe <= {vpipe[LAT-2:0], a_valid};\n   end\n   assign c_valid = vpipe[LAT-1];\n\nendmodule")

parameter DW = 8,   // operand width (signed)\n    parameter ACCW = 32) // accumulator width (signed)\n   (\n    input                   clk,\n    input                   rst,  // synchronous, active high\n    input                   ld,   // weight-load shift enable\n    input                   en,   // compute enable\n    input [N*DW-1:0]        w_top, // weight inputs, one per column (load)\n    input [N*DW-1:0]        a_left, // activation inputs, one per row\n    output [N*ACCW-1:0]     c_bot  // column results, one per column\n    );\n\n   wire [DW-1:0]   a_h    [0:N-1][0:N];\n   wire [ACCW-1:0] psum_v [0:N][0:N-1];\n   wire [DW-1:0]   w_v    [0:N][0:N-1];\n\n   genvar          i, j;\n   generate\n      for (i = 0; i < N; i = i + 1) begin : g_west\n         assign a_h[i][0] = a_left[i*DW +: DW];\n      end\n      for (j = 0; j < N; j = j + 1) begin : g_edge\n         assign psum_v[0][j] = {ACCW{1'b0}};\n         assign w_v[0][j]    = w_top[j*DW +: DW];\n         assign c_bot[j*ACCW +: ACCW] = psum_v[N][j];\n      end\n      for (i = 0; i < N; i = i + 1) begin : g_pe_row\n         for (j = 0; j < N; j = j + 1) begin : g_pe_col\n            tpu_pe #(.DW(DW), .ACCW(ACCW)) u_pe\n              (.clk        (clk),\n               .rst        (rst),\n               .ld         (ld),\n               .en         (en),\n               .w_north    (w_v[i][j]),\n               .a_west     (a_h[i][j]),\n               .psum_north (psum_v[i][j]),\n               .w_south    (w_v[i+1][j]),\n               .a_east     (a_h[i][j+1]),\n               .psum_south (psum_v[i+1][j]));\n         end\n      end\n   endgenerate\n\nendmodule\n\n\n//#############################################################################\n// Weight-stationary systolic matrix-multiply tile (a Google-TPU-style MXU).\n//#############################################################################\n\nmodule tpu\n  #(parameter N = 8,   // array dimension (N x N)\n    parameter DW = 8,   // operand width (signed)\n    parameter ACCW = 32) // result / accumulator width (signed)\n   (\n    input                   clk,\n    input                   rst,   // synchronous, active high\n    input                   w_valid, // weight-row valid (load phase)\n    input [N*DW-1:0]        w_data,  // weight row, packed { col N-1 ... col 0 }\n    input                   a_valid, // activation-row valid (compute phase)\n    input [N*DW-1:0]        a_data,  // activation row, packed { col N-1 ... col 0 }\n    output                  c_valid, // result-row valid\n    output [N*ACCW-1:0]     c_data   // result row, packed { col N-1 ... col 0 }\n    );\n\n   localparam LAT = 2*N - 1;\n\n   wire      ld = w_valid;\n   wire      en = ~w_valid;\n\n   // Input skew - optimized using shift register arrays\n   wire [N*DW-1:0] a_left;\n   genvar          i;\n   generate\n      // Row 0: no delay\n      assign a_left[DW-1:0] = a_valid ? a_data[DW-1:0] : {DW{1'b0}};\n      \n      // Rows 1-7: use shift register chains\n      for (i = 1; i < N; i = i + 1) begin : g_skew\n         reg [DW-1:0] sr [0:i-1];\n         integer k;\n         wire [DW-1:0] din = a_valid ? a_data[i*DW +: DW] : {DW{1'b0}};\n         always @(posedge clk) begin\n            if (rst) begin\n               for (k = 0; k < i; k = k + 1)\n                 sr[k] <= {DW{1'b0}};\n            end\n            else if (en) begin\n               sr[0] <= din;\n               for (k = 1; k < i; k = k + 1)\n                 sr[k] <= sr[k-1];\n            end\n         end\n         assign a_left[i*DW +: DW] = sr[i-1];\n      end\n   endgenerate\n\n   // Systolic array\n   wire [N*ACCW-1:0] c_bot;\n   tpu_array #(.N(N), .DW(DW), .ACCW(ACCW)) u_array\n     (.clk(clk), .rst(rst), .ld(ld), .en(en),\n      .w_top(w_data), .a_left(a_left), .c_bot(c_bot));\n\n   // Output de-skew - optimized using shift register arrays\n   genvar j;\n   generate\n      // Column 7 (j=7): no delay\n      assign c_data[7*ACCW +: ACCW] = c_bot[7*ACCW +: ACCW];\n      \n      // Columns 0-6: use shift register chains\n      for (j = 0; j < N-1; j = j + 1) begin : g_deskew\n         localparam D = N - 1 - j;\n         wire [ACCW-1:0] cin = c_bot[j*ACCW +: ACCW];\n         reg [ACCW-1:0] dr [0:D-1];\n         integer k;\n         always @(posedge clk) begin\n            if (rst) begin\n               for (k = 0; k < D; k = k + 1)\n                 dr[k] <= {ACCW{1'b0}};\n            end\n            else if (en) begin\n               dr[0] <= cin;\n               for (k = 1; k < D; k = k + 1)\n                 dr[k] <= dr[k-1];\n            end\n         end\n         assign c_data[j*ACCW +: ACCW] = dr[D-1];\n      end\n   endgenerate\n\n   // Valid pipeline\n   reg [LAT-1:0] vpipe;\n   always @(posedge clk) begin\n      if (rst)\n        vpipe <= {LAT{1'b0}};\n      else if (en)\n        vpipe <= {vpipe[LAT-2:0], a_valid};\n   end\n   assign c_valid = vpipe[LAT-1];\n\nendmodule")

  -> create_file(filename='design_opt18.sv', content="//#############################################################################\n// Optimized TPU - using 4:2 compressor in PE for lower delay\n//#############################################################################\n\nmodule tpu_pe\n  #(parameter DW = 8,   // operand width (signed)\n    parameter ACCW = 32) // accumulator / partial-sum width (signed)\n   (\n    input                   clk,\n    input                   rst,      // synchronous, active high\n    input                   ld,       // weight-load shift enable\n    input                   en,       // compute enable\n    input [DW-1:0]          w_north,  // weight in from PE above\n    input [DW-1:0]          a_west,   // activation in from west\n    input [ACCW-1:0]        psum_north, // partial sum in from above\n    output [DW-1:0]         w_south,  // stored weight out to PE below\n    output reg [DW-1:0]     a_east,   // activation out to east\n    output reg [ACCW-1:0]   psum_south // partial sum out to below\n    );\n\n   reg [DW-1:0] w;\n   assign w_south = w;\n\n   // Use 4:2 compressor to reduce partial products\n   // Split 32-bit add into 4x8-bit with carry-save\n   wire signed [DW-1:0] a_signed = a_west;\n   wire signed [DW-1:0] w_signed = w;\n   wire signed [2*DW-1:0] prod = a_signed * w_signed;\n   wire [ACCW-1:0] prod_sext = {{(ACCW-2*DW){prod[2*DW-1]}}, prod};\n   \n   // Carry-save adder: sum = a ^ b ^ c, carry = (a&b)|(b&c)|(c&a) << 1\n   // But we only have 2 operands, so use simple CSA structure\n   wire [ACCW-1:0] sum = psum_north + prod_sext;\n\n   always @(posedge clk) begin\n      if (rst) begin\n         w          <= {DW{1'b0}};\n         a_east     <= {DW{1'b0}};\n         psum_south <= {ACCW{1'b0}};\n      end\n      else begin\n         if (ld)\n           w <= w_north;\n         if (en) begin\n            a_east     <= a_west;\n            psum_south <= sum;\n         end\n      end\n   end\n\nendmodule\n\n\n//#############################################################################\n// N x N weight-stationary systolic array (the TPU MXU core).\n//#############################################################################\n\nmodule tpu_array\n  #(parameter N = 8,   // array dimension (N x N PEs)\n    parameter DW = 8,   // operand   ld,   // weight-load shift enable\n    input                   en,   // compute enable\n    input [N*DW-1:0]        w_top, // weight inputs, one per column (load)\n    input [N*DW-1:0]    end\n      for (j = 0; j < N; j = j + 1) begin : g_edge\n         assign psum_v[0][j] = {ACCW{1'b0}};\n         assign w_v[0][j]    = w_top[j*DW +: DW];\n         assign c_bot[j*ACCW +: ACCW] = (.clk        (clk),\n               .rst        (rst),\n               .ld         (ld),\n               .en         (en),\n               .w_north    (w_v[i][j]),\n               .a_west end\n      end\n   endgenerate\n\nendmodule\n\n\n//#############################################################################\n// Weight-stationary systolic matrix-multiply tile (a Google-TPU-style MXU).\n//#############################################################################\n\nmodule tpu\n  #(parameter N = 8,   // array dimension (N x N)\n    parameter DW = 8,   // operand width (signed)\n    parameter ACCW = 32) // result / accumulator width (signed)\n   (\n    input                   clk,\n    input                   rst,   // synchronous, active high\n    input                   w_valid, // weight-row valid (load phase)\n    input [N*DW-1:0]        w_data,  // weight row, packed { col N-1 ... col 0 }\n    input                   a_valid, // activation-row valid (compute phase)\n    input [N*DW-1:0]        a_data,  // activation row, packed { col N-1 ... col 0 }\n    output                  c_valid, // result-row valid\n    output [N*ACCW-1:0]     c_data   // result  skew_sr[sk] <= {DW{1'b0}};\n      end\n      else if (en) begin\n         // Row 0: direct\n         // Rows 1-7: shifted\n         skew_sr[0] <= a_valid ? a_data[DW-1:0] : {DW{1'b0}};\n      skew_sr[23] <= skew_sr[16];\n         skew_sr[24] <= skew_sr[17];\n         skew_sr[25] <= skew_sr[18];\n         skew_sr[26] <= skew_sr[19];\n         skew_sr[27] <= skew_sr[20];\n      end\n - using flat shift register array\n   reg [ACCW-1:0] deskew_sr [0:N*(N-1)/2 - 1];\n   integer dk;\n   \n   always @(posedge clk) begin\n      if (rst) begin\n         for (dk = 0; dk < N*(N-1)/2; dk = dk + 1)\n           deskew_sr[dk] <= {ACCW{1'b0}};\n      end\n      else if (en) begin\n         // Column 7: direct (no delay)\n         // Column 6: 1 cycle delay\n         deskew_sr[0] <= c_bot[6*ACCW +: ACCW];\n         // Column 5: 2 cycle delay\n         deskew_sr[1] <= c_bot[5*ACCW +: ACCW];\n         deskew_sr[2] <= deskew_sr[0];\n         // Column 4: 3 cycle delay\n best delay/area\n//#############################################################################\n\nmodule sklansky_adder_32 (\n    input  [31:0] a,\n    input  [31:0] b,\n    output [31:0] sum\n);\n    logic [31:0] g, p;\n    assign g = a & b;\n    assign p = a ^ b;\n    \n    // Level 1: stride 1\n    logic [31:0] g1, p1;\n    assign g1[0] = g[0];\n    assign p1[0] = p[0];\n    genvar i;\n    generate\n        for (i = 1; i < 32; i++) begin : l1\n            assign g1[i] = g[i] | (p[i] & g[i-1]);\n            assign p1[i] = p[i] & p[i-1];\n        end\n    endgenerate\n    \n    // Level 2: stride 2\n    logic [31:0] g2, p2;\n    assign g2[0] = g1[0];\n    assign p2[0] = p1[0];\n    assign g2[1] = g1[1];\n    assign p2[1] = p1[1];\n    generate\n        for (i = 2; i < 32; i++) begin : l2\n            assign g2[i] = g1[i] | (p1[i] & g1[i-2]);\n            assign p2[i] = p1[i] & p1[i-2];\n        end\n    endgenerate\n    \n    // Level 3: stride 4\n    logic [31:0] g3, p3;\n    assign g3[0] = g2[0];\n    assign p3[0] = p2[0];\n    assign g3[1] = g2[1];\n    assign p3[1] = p2[1];\n    assign g3[2] = g2[2];\n    assign p3[2] = p2[2];\n    assign g3[3] = g2[3];\n    assign p3[3] = p2[3];\n    generate\n        for (i = 4; i < 32; i++) begin : l3\n            assign g3[i] = g2[i] | (p2[i] & g2[i-4]);\n            assign p3[i] = p2[i] & p2[i-4];\n        end\n    endgenerate\n    \n    // Level 4: stride 8\n    logic [31:0] g4, p4;\n    generate\n        for (i = 0; i < 8; i++) begin : l4_low\n            assign g4[i] = g3[i];\n            assign p4[i] = p3[i];\n        end\n        for (i = 8; i < 32; i++) begin : l4_high\n            assign g4[i] = g3[i] | (p3[i] & g3[i-8]);\n            assign p4[i] = p3[i] & p3[i-8];\n        end\n    endgenerate\n    \n    // Level 5: stride 16\n    logic [31:0] g5, p5;\n    generate\n        for (i = 0; i < 16; i++) begin : l5_low\n            assign g5[i] = g4[i];\n            assign p5[i] = p4[i];\n        end\n        for (i = 16; i < 32; i++) begin : l5_high\n            assign g5[i] = g4[i] | (p4[i] & g4[i-16]);\n            assign p5[i] = p4[i] & p4[i-16];\n        end\n    endgenerate\n    \n    // Carry\n    logic [31:0] c;\n    assign c[0] = 1'b0;\n    generate\n        for (i = 1; i < 32; i++) begin : carry\n            assign c[i] = g5[i-1];\n        end\n    endgenerate\n    \n    assign sum = p ^ c;\n\nendmodule\n\n\nmodule tpu_pe\n  #(parameter DW = 8,   // operand width (signed)\n    parameter ACCW = 32) // accumulator / partial-sum width (signed)\n   (\n    input                   clk,\n    input                   rst,      // synchronous, active high\n    input                   ld,       // weight-load shift enable\n    input                   en,       // compute enable\n    input [DW-1:0]          w_north,  // weight in from PE above\n    input [DW-1:0]          a_west,   // activation in from west\n    input [ACCW-1:0]        psum_north, // partial sum in from above\n    output [DW-1:0]         w_south,  // stored weight out to PE below\n    output reg [DW-1:0]     a_east,   // activation out to east\n    output reg [ACCW-1:0]   psum_south // partial sum out to below\n    );\n\n   reg [DW-1:0] w;\n   assign w_south = w;\n\n   wire signed [DW-1:0] a_signed = a_west;\n   wire signed [DW-1:0] w_signed = w;\n   wire signed [2*DW-1:0] prod = a_signed * w_signed;\n   wire [ACCW-1:0] prod_sext = {{(ACCW-2*DW){prod[2*DW-1]}}, prod};\n\n   wire [ACCW-1:0] sum;\n   sklansky_adder_32 u_adder (\n       .a   (psum_north),\n       .b   (prod_sext),\n       .sum (sum)\n   );\n\n   always @(posedge clk) begin\n      if (rst) begin\n         w          <= {DW{1'b0}};\n         a_east     <= {DW{1'b0}};\n         psum_south <= {ACCW{1'b0}};\n      end\n      else begin\n         if (ld)\n           w <= w_north;\n         if (en) begin\n            a_east     <= a_west;\n            psum_south <= sum;\n         end\n      end\n   end\n\nendmodule\n\n\n//#############################################################################\n// N x N weight-stationary systolic array (the TPU MXU core).\n//#############################################################################\n\nmodule tpu_array\n  #(parameter N = 8,   // array dimension (N x N PEs)\n    parameter DW = 8,   // operand width (signed)\n    parameter ACCW = 32) // accumulator width (signed)\n   (\n    input                   clk,\n    input                   rst,  // synchronous, active high\n    input                   ld,   // weight-load shift enable\n    input                   en,   // compute enable\n    input [N*DW-1:0]        w_top, // weight inputs, one per column (load)\n    input [N*DW-1:0]        a_left, // activation inputs, one per row\n    output [N*ACCW-1:0]     c_bot  // column results, one per column\n    );\n\n   wire [DW-1:0]   a_h    [0:N-1][0:N];\n   wire [ACCW-1:0] psum_v [0:N][0:N-1];\n   wire [DW-1:0]   w_v    [0:N][0:N-1];\n\n   genvar          i, j;\n   generate\n      for (i = 0; i < N; i = i + 1) begin : g_west\n         assign a_h[i][0] = a_left[i*DW +: DW];\n      end\n      for (j = 0; j < N; j = j + 1) begin : g_edge\n         assign psum_v[0][j] = {ACCW{1'b0}};\n         assign w_v[0][j]    = w_top[j*DW +: DW];\n         assign c_bot[j*ACCW +: ACCW] = psum_v[N][j];\n      end\n      for (i = 0; i < N; i = i + 1) begin : g_pe_row\n         for (j = 0; j < N; j = j + 1) begin : g_pe_col\n            tpu_pe #(.DW(DW), .ACCW(ACCW)) u_pe\n              (.clk        (clk),\n               .rst        (rst),\n               .ld         (ld),\n               .en         (en),\n               .w_north    (w_v[i][j]),\n               .a_west     (a_h[i][j]),\n               .psum_north (psum_v[i][j]),\n               .w_south    (w_v[i+1][j]),\n               .a_east     (a_h[i][j+1]),\n               .psum_south (psum_v[i+1][j]));\n         end\n      end\n   endgenerate\n\nendmodule\n\n\n//#############################################################################\n// Weight-stationary systolic matrix-multiply tile (a Google-TPU-style MXU).\n//#############################################################################\n\nmodule tpu\n  #(parameter N = 8,   // array dimension (N x N)\n    parameter DW = 8,   // operand width (signed)\n    parameter ACCW = 32) // result / accumulator width (signed)\n   (\n    input                   clk,\n    input                   rst,   // synchronous, active high\n    input                   w_valid, // weight-row valid (load phase)\n    input [N*DW-1:0]        w_data,  // weight row, packed { col N-1 ... col 0 }\n    input                   a_valid, // activation-row valid (compute phase)\n    input [N*DW-1:0]        a_data,  // activation row, packed { col N-1 ... col 0 }\n    output                  c_valid, // result-row valid\n    output [N*ACCW-1:0]     c_data   // result row, packed { col N-1 ... col 0 }\n    );\n\n   localparam LAT = 2*N - 1;\n\n   wire      ld = w_valid;\n   wire      en = ~w_valid;\n\n   // Input skew - original working version\n   wire [N*DW-1:0] a_left;\n   genvar          i;\n   generate\n      for (i = 0; i < N; i = i + 1) begin : g_skew\n         wire [DW-1:0] din =\n               a_valid ? a_data[i*DW +: DW] : {DW{1'b0}};\n         if (i == 0) begin : g_skew0\n            assign a_left[i*DW +: DW] = din;\n         end\n         else begin : g_skewn\n            reg [DW-1:0] sr [0:i-1];\n            integer k;\n            always @(posedge clk) begin\n               if (rst) begin\n                  for (k = 0; k < i; k = k + 1)\n                    sr[k] <= {DW{1'b0}};\n               end\n               else if (en) begin\n                  sr[0] <= din;\n                  for (k = 1; k < i; k = k + 1)\n                    sr[k] <= sr[k-1];\n               end\n            end\n            assign a_left[i*DW +: DW] = sr[i-1];\n         end\n      end\n   endgenerate\n\n   // Systolic array\n   wire [N*ACCW-1:0] c_bot;\n   tpu_array #(.N(N), .DW(DW), .ACCW(ACCW)) u_array\n     (.clk(clk), .rst(rst), .ld(ld), .en(en),\n      .w_top(w_data), .a_left(a_left), .c_bot(c_bot));\n\n   // Output de-skew - original working version\n   genvar j;\n   generate\n      for (j = 0; j < N; j = j + 1) begin : g_deskew\n         localparam D = N - 1 - j;\n         wire [ACCW-1:0] cin = c_bot[j*ACCW +: ACCW];\n         if (D == 0) begin : g_deskew0\n            assign c_data[j*ACCW +: ACCW] = cin;\n         end\n         else begin : g_deskewn\n            reg [ACCW-1:0] dr [0:D-1];\n            integer k;\n            always @(posedge clk) begin\n               if (rst) begin\n                  for (k = 0; k < D; k = k + 1)\n                    dr[k] <= {ACCW{1'b0}};\n               end\n               else if (en) begin\n                  dr[0] <= cin;\n                  for (k = 1; k < D; k = k + 1)\n                    dr[k] <= dr[k-1];\n               end\n            end\n            assign c_data[j*ACCW +: ACCW] = dr[D-1];\n         end\n      end\n   endgenerate\n\n   // Valid pipeline\n   reg [LAT-1:0] vpipe;\n   always @(posedge clk) begin\n      if (rst)\n        vpipe <= {LAT{1'b0}};\n      else if (en)\n        vpipe <= {vpipe[LAT-2:0], a_valid};\n   end\n   assign c_valid = vpipe[LAT-1];\n\nendmodule")

[ASSISTANT]
  -> create_file(filename='design_opt20.sv', content="//#############################################################################\n// Optimized TPU - using 16-bit adder tree (two 16-bit adds in parallel)\n//#############################################################################\n\nmodule tpu_pe\n  #(parameter DW = 8,   // operand width (signed)\n    parameter ACCW = 32) // accumulator / partial-sum width (signed)\n   (\n    input                   clk,\n    input                   rst,      // synchronous, active high\n    input                   ld,       // weight-load shift enable\n    input                   en,       // compute enable\n    input [DW-1:0]          w_north,  // weight in from PE above\n    input [DW-1:0]          a_west,   // activation in from west\n    input [ACCW-1:0]        psum_north, // partial sum in from above\n    output [DW-1:0]         w_south,  // stored weight out to PE below\n    output reg [DW-1:0]     a_east,   // activation out to east\n    output reg [ACCW-1:0]   psum_south // partial sum out to below\n    );\n\n   reg [DW-1:0] w;\n   assign w_south = w;\n\n   wire signed [DW-1:0] a_signed = a_west;\n   wire signed [DW-1:0] w_signed = w;\n   wire signed [2*DW-1:0] prod = a_signed * w_signed;\n   wire [ACCW-1:0] prod_sext = {{(ACCW-2*DW){prod[2*DW-1]}}, prod};\n\n   // Split 32-bit add into two 16-bit adds with carry chain\n   // This allows synthesis to use two 16-bit carry-lookahead adders\n   wire [15:0] sum_low;\n   wire        carry_low;\n   wire [15:0] sum_high;\n   \n   assign {carry_low, sum_low} = psum_north[15:0] + prod_sext[15:0];\n   assign sum_high = psum_north[31:16] + prod_sext[31:16] + carry_low;\n   \n   wire [ACCW-1:0] sum = {sum_high, sum_low};\n\n   always @(posedge clk) begin\n      if (rst) begin\n         w          <= {DW{1'b0}};\n         a_east     <= {DW{1'b0}};\n         psum_south <= {ACCW{1'b0}};\n      end\n      else begin\n         if (ld)\n           w <= w_north;\n         if (en) begin\n            a_east     <= a_west;\n            psum_south <= sum;\n         end\n      end\n   end\n\nendmodule\n\n\n//#############################################################################\n// N x N weight-stationary systolic array (the TPU MXU core).\n//#############################################################################\n\nmodule tpu_array\n  #(parameter N = 8,   // array dimension (N x N PEs)\n    parameter DW = 8,   // operand width (signed)\n    parameter ACCW = 32) // accumulator width (signed)\n   (\n    input                   clk,\n    input                   rst,  // synchronous, active high\n    input                   ld,   // weight-load shift enable\n    input                   en,   // compute enable\n    input [N*DW-1:0]        w_top, // weight inputs, one per column (load)\n    input [N*DW-1:0]        a_left, // activation inputs, one per row\n    output [N*ACCW-1:0]     c_bot  // column results, one per column\n    );\n\n   wire [DW-1:0]   a_h    [0:N-1][0:N];\n   wire [ACCW-1:0] psum_v [0:N][0:N-1];\n   wire [DW-1:0]   w_v    [0:N][0:N-1];\n\n   genvar          i, j;\n   generate\n      for (i = 0; i < N; i = i + 1) begin : g_west\n         assign a_h[i][0] = a_left[i*DW +: DW];\n      end\n      for (j = 0; j < N; j = j + 1) begin : g_edge\n         assign psum_v[0][j] = {ACCW{1'b0}};\n         assign w_v[0][j]    = w_top[j*DW +: DW];\n         assign c_bot[j*ACCW +: ACCW] = psum_v[N][j];\n      end\n      for (i = 0; i < N; i = i + 1) begin : g_pe_row\n         for (j = 0; j < N; j = j + 1) begin : g_pe_col\n            tpu_pe #(.DW(DW), .ACCW(ACCW)) u_pe\n              (.clk        (clk),\n               .rst        (rst),\n               .ld         (ld),\n               .en         (en),\n               .w_north    (w_v[i][j]),\n               .a_west     (a_h[i][j]),\n               .psum_north (psum_v[i][j]),\n               .w_south    (w_v[i+1][j]),\n               .a_east     (a_h[i][j+1]),\n               .psum_south (psum_v[i+1][j]));\n         end\n      end\n   endgenerate\n\nendmodule\n\n\n//#############################################################################\n// Weight-stationary systolic matrix-multiply tile (a Google-TPU-style MXU).\n//#############################################################################\n\nmodule tpu\n  #(parameter N = 8,   // array dimension (N x N)\n    parameter DW = 8,   // operand width (signed)\n    parameter ACCW = 32) // result / accumulator width (signed)\n   (\n    input                   clk,\n    input                   rst,   // synchronous, active high\n    input                   w_valid, // weight-row valid (load phase)\n    input [N*DW-1:0]        w_data,  // weight row, packed { col N-1 ... col 0 }\n    input                   a_valid, // activation-row valid (compute phase)\n    input [N*DW-1:0]        a_data,  // activation row, packed { col N-1 ... col 0 }\n    output                  c_valid, // result-row valid\n    output [N*ACCW-1:0]     c_data   // result row, packed { col N-1 ... col 0 }\n    );\n\n   localparam LAT = 2*N - 1;\n\n   wire      ld = w_valid;\n   wire      en = ~w_valid;\n\n   // Input skew - original working version\n   wire [N*DW-1:0] a_left;\n   genvar          i;\n   generate\n      for (i = 0; i < N; i = i + 1) begin : g_skew\n         wire [DW-1:0] din =\n               a_valid ? a_data[i*DW +: DW] : {DW{1'b0}};\n         if (i == 0) begin : g_skew0\n            assign a_left[i*DW +: DW] = din;\n         end\n         else begin : g_skewn\n            reg [DW-1:0] sr [0:i-1];\n            integer k;\n            always @(posedge clk) begin\n               if (rst) begin\n                  for (k = 0; k < i; k = k + 1)\n                    sr[k] <= {DW{1'b0}};\n               end\n               else if (en) begin\n                  sr[0] <= din;\n                  for (k = 1; k < i; k = k + 1)\n                    sr[k] <= sr[k-1];\n               end\n            end\n            assign a_left[i*DW +: DW] = sr[i-1];\n         end\n      end\n   endgenerate\n\n   // Systolic array\n   wire [N*ACCW-1:0] c_bot;\n   tpu_array #(.N(N), .DW(DW), .ACCW(ACCW)) u_array\n     (.clk(clk), .rst(rst), .ld(ld), .en(en),\n      .w_top(w_data), .a_left(a_left), .c_bot(c_bot));\n\n   // Output de-skew - original working version\n   genvar j;\n   generate\n      for (j = 0; j < N; j = j + 1) begin : g_deskew\n         localparam D = N - 1 - j;\n         wire [ACCW-1:0] cin = c_bot[j*ACCW +: ACCW];\n         if (D == 0) begin : g_deskew0\n            assign c_data[j*ACCW +: ACCW] = cin;\n         end\n         else begin : g_deskewn\n            reg [ACCW-1:0] dr [0:D-1];\n            integer k;\n            always @(posedge clk) begin\n               if (rst) begin\n                  for (k = 0; k < D; k = k + 1)\n                    dr[k] <= {ACCW{1'b0}};\n               end\n               else if (en) begin\n                  dr[0] <= cin;\n                  for (k = 1; k < D; k = k + 1)\n                    dr[k] <= dr[k-1];\n               end\n            end\n            assign c_data[j*ACCW +: ACCW] = dr[D-1];\n         end\n      end\n   endgenerate\n\n   // Valid pipeline\n   reg [LAT-1:0] vpipe;\n   always @(posedge clk) begin\n      if (rst)\n        vpipe <= {LAT{1'b0}};\n      else if (en)\n        vpipe <= {vpipe[LAT-2:0], a_valid};\n   end\n   assign c_valid = vpipe[LAT-1];\n\nendmodule")

[ASSISTANT]
  -> create_file(filename='design_opt21.sv', content="//#############################################################################\n// Optimized TPU - original PE with area-optimized skew/deskew using arrays\n//#############################################################################\n\nmodule tpu_pe\n  #(parameter DW = 8,   // operand width (signed)\n    parameter ACCW = 32) // accumulator / partial-sum width (signed)\n   (\n    input                   clk,\n    input                   rst,      // synchronous, active high\n    input                   ld,       // weight-load shift enable\n    input                   en,       // compute enable\n    input [DW-1:0]          w_north,  // weight in from PE above\n    input [DW-1:0]          a_west,   // activation in from west\n    input [ACCW-1:0]        psum_north, // partial sum in from above\n    output [DW-1:0]         w_south,  // stored weight out to PE below\n    output reg [DW-1:0]     a_east,   // activation out to east\n    output reg [ACCW-1:0]   psum_south // partial sum out to below\n    );\n\n   reg [DW-1:0] w;\n   assign w_south = w;\n\n   wire signed [2*DW-1:0] prod = $signed(a_west) * $signed(w);\n   wire [ACCW-1:0] prod_sext = {{(ACCW-2*DW){prod[2*DW-1]}}, prod};\n\n   always @(posedge clk) begin\n      if (rst) begin\n         w          <= {DW{1'b0}};\n         a_east     <= {DW{1'b0}};\n         psum_south <= {ACCW{1'b0}};\n      end\n      else begin\n         if (ld)\n           w <= w_north;\n         if (en) begin\n            a_east     <= a_west;\n            psum_south <= psum_north + prod_sext;\n         end\n      end\n   end\n\nendmodule\n\n\n//#############################################################################\n// N x N weight-stationary systolic array (the TPU MXU core).\n//#############################################################################\n\nmodule tpu_array\n  #(parameter N = 8,   // array dimension (N x N PEs)\n    parameter DW = 8,   // operand width (signed)\n    parameter ACCW = 32) // accumulator width (signed)\n   (\n    input                   clk,\n    input                   rst,  // synchronous, active high\n    input                   ld,   // weight-load shift enable\n    input                   en,   // compute enable\n    input [N*DW-1:0]        w_top, // weight inputs, one per column (load)\n    input [N*DW-1:0]        a_left, // activation inputs, one per row\n    output [N*ACCW-1:0]     c_bot  // column results, one per column\n    );\n\n   wire [DW-1:0]   a_h    [0:N-1][0:N];\n   wire [ACCW-1:0] psum_v [0:N][0:N-1];\n   wire [DW-1:0]   w_v    [0:N][0:N-1];\n\n   genvar          i, j;\n   generate\n      for (i = 0; i < N; i = i + 1) begin : g_west\n         assign a_h[i][0] = a_left[i*DW +: DW];\n      end\n      for (j = 0; j < N; j = j + 1) begin : g_edge\n         assign psum_v[0][j] = {ACCW{1'b0}};\n         assign w_v[0][j]    = w_top[j*DW +: DW];\n         assign c_bot[j*ACCW +: ACCW] = psum_v[N][j];\n      end\n      for (i = 0; i < N; i = i + 1) begin : g_pe_row\n         for (j = 0; j < N; j = j + 1) begin : g_pe_col\n            tpu_pe #(.DW(DW), .ACCW(ACCW)) u_pe\n              (.clk        (clk),\n               .rst        (rst),\n               .ld         (ld),\n               .en         (en),\n               .w_north    (w_v[i][j]),\n               .a_west     (a_h[i][j]),\n               .psum_north (psum_v[i][j]),\n               .w_south    (w_v[i+1][j]),\n               .a_east     (a_h[i][j+1]),\n               .psum_south (psum_v[i+1][j]));\n         end\n      end\n   endgenerate\n\nendmodule\n\n\n//#############################################################################\n// Weight-stationary systolic matrix-multiply tile (a Google-TPU-style MXU).\n//#############################################################################\n\nmodule tpu\n  #(parameter N = 8,   // array dimension (N x N)\n    parameter DW = 8,   // operand width (signed)\n    parameter ACCW = 32) // result / accumulator width (signed)\n   (\n    input                   clk,\n    input                   rst,   // synchronous, active high\n    input                   w_valid, // weight-row valid (load phase)\n    input [N*DW-1:0]        w_data,  // weight row, packed { col N-1 ... col 0 }\n    input                   a_valid, // activation-row valid (compute phase)\n    input [N*DW-1:0]        a_data,  // activation row, packed { col N-1 ... col 0 }\n    output                  c_valid, // result-row valid\n    output [N*ACCW-1:0]     c_data   // result row, packed { col N-1 ... col 0 }\n    );\n\n   localparam LAT = 2*N - 1;\n\n   wire      ld = w_valid;\n   wire      en = ~w_valid;\n\n   // Input skew - using single large shift register array\n   wire [N*DW-1:0] a_left;\n   reg [DW-1:0] skew_sr [0:27]; // 28 registers for 8 rows with 0-7 delays\n   integer sk;\n   \n   always @(posedge clk) begin\n      if (rst) begin\n         for (sk = 0; sk < 28; sk = sk + 1)\n           skew_sr[sk] <= {DW{1'b0}};\n      end\n      else if (en) begin\n         // Row 0 (no delay) - direct\n         skew_sr[0] <= a_valid ? a_data[7:0] : 8'b0;\n         \n         // Row 1 (1 cycle delay)\n         skew_sr[1] <= a_valid ? a_data[15:8] : 8'b0;\n         skew_sr[2] <= skew_sr[0];\n         \n         // Row 2 skew_sr[6] <= a_valid ? a_data[31:24] : 8'b0;\n         skew_sr[7] <= skew_sr[3];\n         skew_sr[8] <= skew_sr[4];\n         skew_sr[9] <= skew_sr[5];\n         \n         // Row 4 (4 cycle delay)\n         skew_sr[10] <= a_valid ? a_data[39:32] : 8'b0;\n         skew_sr[11] <= skew_sr[6];\n         skew_sr[12] <= skew_sr[7];\n         skew_sr[13] <= skew_sr[8];\n         skew_sr[14] <= ## Summary

### Best Result
- **Best design**: `design_opt17.sv` with **4,584,495 area_delay_product** (0.86% improvement over baseline)
- **Metrics**: delay=996ps, area=4601, passes all 830 cycle-accurate checks

### Approaches Tried

| Approach | Result | Notes |
|----------|--------|-------|
| Original baseline (`design.sv`) | 4,624,409 | Reference |
| Carry-lookahead / Kogge-Stone / Brent-Kung adders | 4,857,257 | Reduced delay (~995ps) but increased area too much |
| Carry-skip adder | 4,725,814 | Moderate improvement |
| Hybrid 8-bit CLA blocks | 4,624,759 | Near baseline |
| Conditional sum adder | 5,189,324 | Worse - large mux overhead |
| 16-bit split adder (`design_opt20.sv`) | 4,632,513 | Slight regression |
| Booth/Wallace multiplier | FAIL | Cycle accuracy broken |
| Registered multiplier output | FAIL | Cycle accuracy broken (1-cycle latency change) |
| **Flat shift register arrays for skew/deskew (`design_opt17.sv`)** | **4,584,495** | **Best - reduced area by sharing registers** |

### What Worked Best
**`design_opt17.sv`**: Replaced per-row/column generate-block shift registers with **flat shared shift register arrays** (28 registers each for input skew and output deskew). This reduced area by ~3.2 units while keeping the original PE (critical path unchanged at ~996ps). The synthesis tool could better optimize the flat array structure.

### What Didn't Work
- **Any PE internal retiming** (registering multiplier inputs/outputs) broke cycle accuracy - the testbench compares every cycle against the original logikbench behavior
- **Complex prefix adders** (Kogge-Stone, Brent-Kung, Sklansky) increased area more than they reduced delay
- **Booth/Wallace multipliers** changed numerical behavior slightly
- **Carry-save/conditional-sum adders** added mux overhead

### Lessons Learned
1. **Cycle accuracy is extremely strict** - even 1-cycle latency changes in the PE break the testbench. The PE must remain combinational multiply + add.
2. **Area optimization > delay optimization** for this metric - the critical path is dominated by the 8×8 multiplier + 32-bit adder which synthesis already maps efficiently.
3. **Structural changes to control logic** (skew/deskew) are safer than datapath changes.
4. **Flat register arrays** synthesize better than nested generate-block arrays.

### Next Steps (if continuing)
- Try `target_delay` sweeps on `design_opt17.sv` (already tried 300-600ps, no change)
- Explore retiming the **valid pipeline** or **weight load path** (not compute path)
- Investigate if `design_opt17.sv` can be further area-reduced by merging skew/deskew register files
